bpf\+\_\+prog\+\_\+select\+\_\+runtime -\/ select exec runtime for B\+PF program @fp\+: \mbox{\hyperlink{structbpf__prog}{bpf\+\_\+prog}} populated with internal B\+PF program @err\+: pointer to error variable

Try to J\+IT e\+B\+PF program, if J\+IT is not available, use interpreter. \mbox{\hyperlink{classThe}{The}} B\+PF program will be executed via \mbox{\hyperlink{linux_2filter_8h_a0c94afc7cda78d0a476ce12c957953e0}{bpf\+\_\+prog\+\_\+run()}} function.

Return\+: the \&fp argument along with \&err set to 0 for success or a negative errno code on failure Here is the call graph for this function\+:
